HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003","root_001281/B010","root_001564/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0015a774bda9d16a09d0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:12:2\",\"root_000880\",[\"B003\"]],[\"87:12:3\",\"root_001564\",[\"B002\"]],[\"87:12:4\",\"root_001281\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Büyük ateş, 87:13'te ölüm ve yaşam yolları kapanınca boşaltılamayan ağır bir yük niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001","root_001564/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0ae799a3bafb13189616","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:12:3\",\"root_001564\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateşin ışık yönü, öğütten kaçınırken saklanmak istenen iç yönelişi yakarak görünür kılan bir açığa çıkarma işlevi görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0bb296f04a0a384785a7","text":"2. **Fire-straightening as punitive correction.** Lexical evidence: ص ل ي B004 (kindling, roasting, fire-straightening), س و ي B002 in 87:2 (straight completion), ز ك و B002 in 87:14 (purification). Structural cue: early divine proportioning and later self-purification contrast with fire contact. Assigned role: fire becomes a destructive straightening for what refused formative ease. Abductive move: what is not corrected by reminder may be exposed to a harsher corrective heat. Reading change: \"burns\" also hints at failed formation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0c0acf5fa6e0bc01957f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:12:2\",\"root_000880\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ateşte işlenip doğrultulma çağrışımı, 87:14-15'teki gönüllü arınma ve namazın karşısına zorlayıcı bir dönüşüm çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0ec3dc12fdbfacc9a12b","text":"3. **Ritual prayer's negative twin.** Lexical evidence: صلي B001 ritual prayer and B003 fire exposure; صلو B003 prescribed worship in 87:15. Structural cue: the same ṣ-l-y field later marks successful prayer. Assigned role: the root holds two possible orientations: worshipful prayer or fire-contact. Abductive move: the packet supplies both branches; I infer contrast because 87:12 and 87:15 occupy opposite destinies. Reading change: the ayah becomes a reversal: one who does not enter prayer enters fire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_18e76104a8824829331c","text":"87:13 changed `ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ` most sharply: the fire is “great” as an undischarged burden once both death and life are denied. The later `تَزَكَّىٰ` and `فَصَلَّىٰ` in 87:14–15 also exposed the contrasting gentler route, with the `يَصْلَى/صَلَّى` sound echo separating compelled heat from chosen worship.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1dc0d5e1e1f9a5f1b3c3","text":"1. **The great fire is an undischarged burden.** `ص ل ي/B003` gives encountering and suffering fire, `ن و ر/B002` gives burning fire, and `ك ب ر/B010` gives greatness as heaviness or hardship. The relative clause continues the avoider of 87:11, while 87:13 removes both death and life as exits. Fire supplies exposure, and `ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ` supplies a load whose magnitude is experiential rather than merely spatial. I infer that the following non-termination prevents discharge of the burden. The phrase reads as sustained, overwhelming liability, not only a very large flame.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_23b464cb50e45e829100","text":"87:14-15 made the fire scene more corrective by contrast: purification and prayer offer a non-burning way to be set right. 87:7 made fire's light a mode of exposure after hiddenness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_243e975996c43070747c","text":"87:15 made the ṣ-l-y reversal unmistakable: the same broad root-field can appear as prayer for the purified or fire for the avoidant. 87:17 also revised \"الكبرى\": the truly better and more lasting is not the larger fire but the afterlife's خير/أبقى.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B001","root_000880/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3d4e121ef5d302115e86","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:12:2\",\"root_000880\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateşe girme ile 87:15'teki namaz arasındaki ses ve anlam yakınlığı, öğüde verilen iki karşıt yönelişi görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_444507dbcf5402252ce2","text":"4. **The \"greater\" as counterfeit highness.** Lexical evidence: كبر B006 grandeur/self-magnification; علو B001/B002 rightful height in 87:1; دنو B002 lower/nearer life in 87:16. Structural cue: الأعلى at the opening and الكبرى here form a scale contrast. Assigned role: الكبرى marks a terrifying magnitude that answers false preference. Abductive move: the packet supplies true elevation and heavy greatness; I infer that \"greater\" is not better but more burdensome. Reading change: the superlative force of the surah splits into the Highest Lord and the greater fire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4dbbfd27afa4368e938f","text":"2. **Fire as false illumination against guidance.** Lexical evidence: نور B001 light/illumination and B002 burning fire; هدي B001 guidance in 87:3; علم B002 signs/marks in 87:7. Structural cue: the surah has already offered guidance and knowledge; the avoidant meets a different \"light.\" Assigned role: النار is illumination converted into burning exposure. Abductive move: the packet supplies light/fire duality; I infer that refused guidance returns as harmful visibility. Reading change: the fire is a dark parody of guidance-light.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5fbd12541b03d99fe814","text":"1. **Entering the greater fire as exposure to heat.** Lexical evidence: صلي B003 encountering fire/heat; نور B002 burning fire; كبر B001 greatness and B010 heaviness/hardship. Structural cue: the relative clause defines الأشقى by his fire-outcome. Assigned role: صلي supplies exposure/entry; النار supplies burning illumination; الكبرى intensifies scale and burden. Abductive move: the packet supplies heat and greatness; I infer punitive immersion from the syntax. Reading change: the ayah reads as entering a maximal heat-field, not merely seeing fire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_64da9ea14977f9b14e31","text":"1. **Fire as the hard endpoint of avoidance.** Lexical evidence: ص ل ي B003 (encountering fire and heat), ن و ر B002 (burning fire/branding), ك ب ر B001/B010 (greatness, hardship/heaviness). Structural cue: the relative clause defines الأشقى from 87:11. Assigned role: صلي names direct exposure; نار supplies burning illumination; كبرى marks the maximal burden. Abductive move: avoidance of reminder does not escape exposure but relocates the person into a harsher encounter. Reading change: the fire is the embodied consequence of sidestepping.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_83b80fcab12734a9d3f9","text":"2. **Burning also becomes exposure.** Alongside fire, `ن و ر/B001` gives illumination, and the earlier `جَهْر/خَفِي` opposition in 87:7 distinguishes manifestation from concealment. The person who moved aside from reminder is now placed in a medium whose root both burns and makes visible. Illumination’s role is to reveal the orientation that avoidance attempted to keep outside the address. I infer disclosure as a function of the fire; the ayah explicitly supplies heat, not an act of diagnosis. The fire can therefore read as punitive manifestation as well as combustion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001281/B006","root_001281/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b62f36ce312b3a45dc7a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:12:4\",\"root_001281\",[\"B006\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Büyüklük, Rabbin haklı yüceliğine rakip olan insan büyüklüğünün sonunda ağır ve yıkıcı bir karşılığa dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000880/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c893dd1f7158dd0b8781","text":"3. **Fire is a coercive transformation contrasted with chosen purification.** `ص ل ي/B004` includes heating a thing to straighten or process it. Later 87:14 gives `ز ك و/B002`, voluntary purification, and 87:15 gives `ص ل و/B003`, prescribed worship, while the close sound-pattern `يَصْلَى/صَلَّى` sharpens the fork. The fixed fire branch supplies forced material alteration; the later roots supply self-directed purification and ritual alignment. I infer a corrective function from the straightening image, not from an explicit causal statement. The ayah reads as the hard transformation on the route that rejected the gentler one.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f238e216ce28f8c7751f","text":"3. **Light reversed into fire.** Lexical evidence: ن و ر B001/B002 (illumination and fire), ج ه ر B002 in 87:7 (plain manifestation), خ ف ي B001 (hiddenness). Structural cue: what is hidden/open is known before the fire scene. Assigned role: fire is a terrifying manifestation, a light that exposes by burning. Abductive move: disclosure can be merciful as reminder or punitive as flame.
